- **What changed.** `engine/exit_codes.js` (new): one exit-code classifier, adopted by `engine/register_reality.js` and `engine/wire_ladder.js`. The mutation harness examines class-A siblings and counts mutated citations as skipped rather than scored, each behind a knob; `engine/quarantine.js`, `engine/divergence_report.js` and `engine/divergence_cards.js` fix the open-defect and card defects; seven COULD-NOT-STAGE paths across four probe files now report that they cannot answer instead of exiting 0. `data/mutation-coverage.json` is regraded by a new mode that plays no games.
- **Measured.** Each fix took its probe from red to green on the current tree and back to red on revert: #323, #325, #349, #350, #399, #380 (second part) and #524. Regrade: operators 1,563 to 1,546, read-and-ignored 998 to 981, class A 148 to 146 — published by no living document. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-instrument-probe-fixes.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Owed as work: `tests/run-all.js` adopting the shared classifier and the #380 probe's regex widened (engine agent); #348 re-scoped, since Git Bash damages the arguments before the wrapper sees them; the ROADMAP cells for these rows, applied once the engine agent's registration lands.


Three rules about the figures in a row, all of them already enforced elsewhere:

- **Cite the artifact by name.** A figure attributed to an artifact that does not contain it fails
  the citation clause of the documentation gate, on this page exactly as on the white paper.
- **Write a retraction as a strikethrough with the word `retracted` on the same line.** That is the
  form the derived retraction registry reads. Once a row here retracts a figure, any living document
  still stating it as fact fails the build — which is the point: **deferring the documentation pass
  never defers a retraction.** The stale number comes OUT of the white paper in this pass; only the
  rewrite waits for the major.
- **A quarantined figure is not written here at all.** A caption is not a quarantine.
- **`Basis.` is a JUDGEMENT and is written as one.** Absent reads as `unchanged` and prints as
  `[basis not stated]` in `node engine/docs_scan.js --owed`, so the omission is visible rather than
  silent. Declaring `CHANGED` obliges the release to be `X.0.0` with the full document set folded in,
  and clause 5d fails the build otherwise.

---

## [6.14.1] — 2026-09-11 — the faint epoch is per battle, so a double wipe's winner survives a later battle being built

- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: `battleInit` records `S._faintEpoch`; `battleTurn` makes it the active epoch, so `noteFaint` stamps a corpse with the epoch of the battle being stepped; `battleResult` compares against the state's own epoch. A state `battleInit` never stamped falls back to the process epoch and is counted (`MEDFAILS.faintEpochUnstamped`). Knob `MEDI_FAINT_EPOCH_GLOBAL=1` restores the process-global rule and stamps `MEDFAILS.faintEpochGlobalRestored`. Release `aefcb93baf14`.
- **Measured.** The interleave bench's double-wipe case, same flags as its first run: on release `cfab4786683a` (the leak), `double_wipes.struggle_fixture.after_one_build_differs` 43 of 43, the natural late read differs 1 of 1 (`data/verification/interleave-2026-09-11/interleave-cfab4786683a.json`); on `aefcb93baf14`, 0 of 43 and 0 of 1 (`data/verification/interleave-2026-09-11/interleave-aefcb93baf14.json`); on `aefcb93baf14` under the knob, 43 of 43 and 1 of 1 again (`data/verification/interleave-2026-09-11/interleave-aefcb93baf14-knob-global.json`). Nothing else moved on the new release: `data/engine-diff.json` disagreed 0 of 6000; `data/mechanics-census.json` 872 live, 0 missing.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. `MID_NTH` (the event-dice repeat counter) still carries no battle identity; the differential plays one game at a time and is unaffected.

## [6.14.0] — 2026-09-11 — the staged harness plays the planner's fixtures, gender reaches a real game, scope is decided in one place, and eight new partings are registered

- **What changed.** `engine/all_mechanics_fire.js` plays `engine/stage_planner.js` fixtures: abilities and items A/B the fixture against its one-leaf control on every rendered variant (near, far, slot b), with the legacy ladder as a named fallback; moves keep the legacy ladder and also play the planner fixture. `engine/game_differential.js` `buildPair` gains two opt-in seams, `declaredSpread` and `declaredGender`; medicham2 writes a declared gender into `switch`, `drag` and `detailschange` details. `engine/stage_planner.js` declares a legal gender pair where a handler reads gender (its report's claim that it already did was measured false) and plays the real HP pool where the carrier mega-evolves or changes forme (the x6 pool had parted 73 stones and 13 mega abilities on HP at about six times — one instrument artifact). `engine/fixture_preflight.js` reads `?.` as `.` (Prankster, Gale Wings, Pickpocket, Triage gained their need; four, exactly). Scope: the harness, `tests/roster.js` and `engine/tag_dex.js` ask `engine/legal_scope.js`; `champions_sim.unreachable()` is deleted. `data/tags.json` drops Battle Bond's row and nothing else. Releases `cfab4786683a`, then `aefcb93baf14`. Prediction first: `data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-11-integration.json`. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-integration.md`.

The prediction was 795 in 778 to 812; items and moves landed on the point, abilities below it.
- **Measured, the harness artifact.** `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` on release `aefcb93baf14`: 4714 games, 0 threw; `summary` diverged moves 5, abilities 8, items 3; `planner.played` 3836 games, `planner.seam.declaredGender` 20.
- **Measured, the pinned pool — unmoved, as predicted.** `data/game-differential.json` state.games 961, state.games_board_never_diverged 961; `data/verification/game-differential-top-tie-first.json` 961 and 960; `data/verification/game-differential-bottom-tie-first.json` 961 and 959. `data/engine-diff.json` disagreed 0 of 6000. The three roster stages 0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER; the abilities stage now counts 200 in scope, its own derivation having counted 201. `data/mechanics-census.json` 872 live, 872 probed, 0 missing.
- **Found, registered, not fixed.** Board-material: Oblivious lets Taunt through (#593), Magic Bounce does not bounce Spite (#594), a Super Luck or Scope Lens holder misses the crit after Focus Energy at the top corner (#595), a mega stone held under Klutz does not evolve here (#596). Narration: Cute Charm (#597), Own Tempo (#598), Sweet Veil (#599), Covet (#600). Gender (#592) is closed. `engine/quarantine.js`: GATE CLOSED, 1 of 9 — the mechanics clause, 9 of 16 diverging mechanics played and uncleared, every one of them among these eight.
- **Supersedes.** ~~740 of 845~~ retracted — staged mechanics that fired and fired with a board compared, as `engine/coverage.js` printed them into the generated status blocks; restamped by `node engine/status.js --write` in this pass. And the deliberate roster's scope figures, which the one scope moved: ~~201 in scope~~ retracted and ~~14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET~~ retracted (abilities), ~~498 in scope~~ retracted and ~~487 tested~~ retracted (moves) — they stood in `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` and `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and are replaced there in this pass by 200, 13, 497 and 486, read off `data/roster.abilities.json` and `data/roster.moves.json` (release aefcb93baf14).

## [6.10.1] — 2026-09-11 — a notes row committed before a same-day regeneration is reported, not judged: the dated-block check orders a tie by the clock

- **What changed.** `engine/docs_scan.js`: `citationMismatches` and `untraceableCensus` decided "the cited artifact was regenerated after the block" by DATE, so a row and a regeneration on the same day tied and the row was judged against bytes written after it. A tie is now ordered by the committer instant `git blame` records for the figure's line against the artifact's `generated` instant (`regeneratedAfter`); an uncommitted line, or a stamp with no time or no zone, is still judged, and the instant never overrides a date. Eight demonstration cases were added.
- **Measured.** Old rule against new on the same bytes: exactly two figures moved from judged to reported, rows 134 and 144 of this page; none moved the other way; the census is unchanged. The proofs were red on the old rule and are green after, with a same-day control committed after the regeneration still judged. Gate 37 of 37, baseline unmoved. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-dated-block-granularity.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Still open: the gate reads working-tree artifacts, not the commit being made.

## [6.10.0] — 2026-09-11 — which mechanics exist in the regulation is decided in one place, and the planner's in-scope count was wrong on three rows in two directions

- **What changed.** `engine/legal_scope.js` is the one implementation of scope: `verdict(kind, id)` returns in or out of scope with a code (CARRIED, LEARNED, INJECTED, CONFERRED, HELD; or NO-LEGAL-CARRIER, VALIDATOR-REFUSED, NO-LEGAL-READER). `engine/stage_planner.js` imports it and deletes its own carrier-list scope and conferral scan; `engine/coverage.js` lists out-of-scope rows by code; `tests/test-stage-planner.js` clause `oneScope` fails when the planner or the coverage counter returns a different in-scope set.
- **Measured.** The two derivations disagreed on three rows, found as a set difference: Battle Bond is out (its only carrier is refused by the TeamValidator), Simple is in (Simple Beam is legal and the validator accepts a holder), Gluttony is out (the only readers of its flag are 14 banned berries). 845 of 964 mechanics are in scope, the same total as the coverage counter's with two members swapped; the planner's fixtures stay at 841, now of 845. The clause was red on the pre-change code (6 failures) and on a planted second derivation. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-scope-unified.md`.
- **Supersedes.** The in-scope denominator in the 6.9.1 row and CHANGELOG 6.9.1, which counted Battle Bond and Gluttony and left out Simple; the correct denominator is 845.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Owed as work: `engine/all_mechanics_fire.js`, `tests/roster.js`, `engine/tag_dex.js` and `champions_sim.unreachable()` still decide scope their own way.

## [6.9.2] — 2026-09-11 — copying a mid-game position costs about a third of one turn in MEDICHAM, and its copies are independent

- **What changed.** Added a copy-cost bench under `data/verification/clone-cost-2026-09-11/`, run on frozen release 2b5a6585d8cf and the pinned Showdown checkout over 48 positions from the pinned pool at turn depths 2, 5, 8 and 12, with each copy interleaved against a turn play in one process.
- **Measured.** A copy costs 0.32 of one turn in MEDICHAM and 0.44 in Showdown's string round trip, median over all depths; copy plus three turns is 2.52 turns in MEDICHAM and 3.28 in Showdown; the copy is about 6% of a full rollout in both, so playing turns, not copying positions, limits search. MEDICHAM's rebuild-from-board copy is independent on all 48 positions; Showdown's in-memory JSON copy is not — it shares the original's log and fails every check on all 48. Taken under contention, so the ratios are the figures of record. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-clone-cost.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Owed as work: a quiet-machine re-run for absolute times, and a check that two live MEDICHAM positions played alternately stay independent, since the engine keeps its current battle in shared module variables.

## [6.9.1] — 2026-09-11 — a staging planner derives a legal fixture for 841 of the 847 mechanics with a legal carrier

- **What changed.** Added `engine/stage_planner.js` and `tests/test-stage-planner.js`. For every legal move, ability and item — and abilities a legal move can confer — the planner derives what the mechanic needs from the authority's handlers and the tag params, then constructs a legal board that supplies it, with a control differing in one leaf and inert for one reason, on both sides and at the slot where the mechanic reads position. No game is played.
- **Measured.** 841 of 964 mechanics carry a validated fixture, 841 of the 847 with a legal carrier; 1,359 distinct teams pass Showdown's TeamValidator. 123 refused with codes: no legal carrier 117, needs engine capability 3 (gender, pinned by the driver at `engine/game_differential.js:3412`, not by medicham2), validator refused 1, no legal reader 1, planner cannot construct 1. The test is green, and all 13 of its checks were shown red on a broken planner. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-stage-planner.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Owed as work: integrate into `engine/all_mechanics_fire.js` once the engine batch holding the game slot lands; an opt-in declared spread for 13 speed-dependent fixtures; `engine/fixture_preflight.js` cannot read a move's category (Prankster, Gale Wings, Pickpocket, Triage).

## [6.8.1] — 2026-09-11 — a grandfathered figure leaves the list only when a document binds it, and MODELS.md's 925 is bound to the commit that wrote it

- **What changed.** `engine/docs_scan.js`: a grandfathered figure that some file on disk happens to contain now stays on the list as dormant; it retires only when its sentence or value is edited, or when a binding a document wrote holds it — the CHANGELOG entry its block names, or a commit-pinned blob. A commit-pinned citation (a commit hash, a colon, then a data path and an optional field) is read from git as a bound trace, not as a citation of today's file. `docs/MODELS.md:1493` binds its held-out figure to the commit that produced it. `tests/test-docs-current.js` writes the list through `retainGrandfathered`, and its bound-trace demonstration grows from 16 to 23 cases.
- **Measured.** The gate was red on MODELS.md's 925: the bootstrap had read an older meta-usage artifact in which an unrelated species threat count equals 925, so the figure was bound by coincidence and never grandfathered, and the next ingest removed it. The commit that carried that ingest in through a rebase was never gated on its own tree, because the pre-commit hook skips a rebase. The first green run after the bootstrap retired two white-paper figures nobody had edited, and a third was one run from the same. The old rule fails the new flaw case, a keep-while-unedited rule fails both controls, and the shipped rule passes every case; the list goes from 2,256 to 2,249, all seven retired by the new pin; gate 37 of 37. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-ratchet-925.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None — no published figure moved. Still unprotected: 1,317 figures bound only by a file on disk and on no list, which an artifact regeneration can still turn red; and the hook's rebase skip.

## [6.6.2] — 2026-09-11 — the untraceable-figure check is a ratchet: a figure is traced only by a trace bound to it, 2,258 grandfathered

- **What changed.** `engine/docs_scan.js` `untraceableCensus`: a figure is traced only by an artifact its own paragraph cites that holds it (narrowed to the named field when the citation names one) or by the CHANGELOG entry its block names; a hit anywhere in the data directory or anywhere in the CHANGELOG is no longer a trace. QUALIFIED sentences and sentences citing only a missing file now need a trace too, and a citation of the open-work or baseline artifact counts for nothing, so citing no longer costs a figure. `tests/test-docs-current.js` ratchets a generated grandfather list, written once by `node tests/test-docs-current.js --bootstrap-grandfather`; it may only shrink, and a grandfathered figure whose value or sentence changes loses its place.
- **Measured.** 2,258 figures grandfathered across 24 living documents; 1,325 bound through a cited artifact and 3,816 through a named entry; the untraceable census unchanged at 22 across 4 — `data/docs-currency-baseline.json`. Gate 37 of 37. Plants: a new unbound figure RED, a field-bound figure green and RED one digit off, a grandfathered figure edited RED, a CHANGELOG-only figure under the wrong version RED — the first re-run independently. Detail: `docs/_reports/2026-09-11-traceability-ratchet.md`.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None — no published figure moved. Binding the 2,258 grandfathered figures is separate burn-down work, and binding through a cited artifact is still weak: about 726 of its 1,325 binds would happen by chance, 560 of them through the whole-game differential artifact, so a field-scoped bind is the next tightening.

## [6.6.1] — 2026-09-11 — the two corrupted-winner ladder rows are excluded by declaration, as Will decided

- **What changed.** `data/quality-filter.json` 1.3.0 -> 1.4.0 adds the rule `exclude_corrupt_winner` (reason code `corrupt_winner`) naming the two #558 ids — `gen9championsvgc2026regmb-2662690089` and `gen9championsvgc2026regmb-2672145722` — with their defect, players and re-fetch receipts. `engine/quality.js` and `engine/quality.py` apply it as the last funnel stage. It is a DECLARATION, not a filter: nothing tests whether a winner is a player, and `engine/sanity_check.py`'s winner clause still counts every bad winner and fails on an UNDECLARED one or on a declared id whose winner has since been corrected, so the declaration cannot outlive its defect (shown red three ways on a scratch copy first). No store byte written.
- **Measured.** `python engine/sanity_check.py` 94 passed / 2 failed -> 95 passed / 1 failed (the remaining failure is #550, a side bringing six). `tests/test-quality.js` 35/0, JS and Python selecting 32,192 games. The clean ladder corpus drops by ONE game, 32,193 -> 32,192 — one of the two rows was already excluded for a partial bring. Store still 92,594 ladder rows, same bytes.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure is re-derived here.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None. Owed as work: the release digest moved (`engine/quality.js` and `data/quality-filter.json` are frozen SOURCES), so every gate clause reads "measured against a different engine" until the next ENGINE batch re-measures; the differential's pool reads bo3 and ots only and neither id is in it, so no gate figure can move. A hash-bound store-correction mechanism remains owed (OPS). #550's cause is the fetch (a UTF-8 character split across HTTP chunks), not the parser.

## [6.6.0] — 2026-09-11 — Will decides three open questions, and the #558 store repair is refused because the store cannot express a correction

- **What changed.** Will answered three questions put to him on 2026-09-11. (1) **The untraceable-figure check will be RATCHETED**: a new or changed figure must be bound to its source (a named artifact and key, or the CHANGELOG entry its block names); existing figures are grandfathered, counted, and burned down. Measured first: every 3-digit integer (900 of 900) and 7,274 of 9,000 four-digit integers already occur somewhere in `data/` (`engine/docs_scan.js` `artifactNumbers` over 306 artifacts), so the check could not fail for most figures. Writing an orphaned figure into CHANGELOG as its "trace", done three times on 2026-09-10, is the same unbound digit match and is stopped. (2) **The two corrupted-winner ladder rows (#558) are to be EXCLUDED now** and a correction mechanism built later. (3) **`engine/em_validation.js` is WAIVED with the MAG group** — entry added to `data/test-waivers.json` recording his selected option.
- **Measured.** #558 repair REFUSED by MEASURE: `build/compress-stores.js` cannot express a correction — first-occurrence-wins keeps the corrupt row and raw log in every route, a correction written as a new parsed shard trips the shrink guard and would stop the ingest Action, and `--check` compares ids and counts, not content. Both replays re-fetch clean (receipts in `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-corrupted-winners.md`). No store byte written; 92,594 ladder rows before and after.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None directly. Owed as work: the ratchet in `engine/docs_scan.js`; the #558 exclusion; a hash-bound store-correction mechanism (OPS, later); #558's VERIFIED BY should read "winner clause 2 -> 0", and #550's "parser, not store" wording is wrong — the cause is the fetch.

## [6.1.0] — 2026-09-10 — the damage differential runs the authority's volley loop, and `0 of 6000` stops being a claim about single-hit moves

- **What changed.** `tests/test-engine-diff.js` entered the authority at `battle.actions.moveHit`, and
  one `moveHit` call is ONE ARRIVAL — so every multi-hit move and every Parental Bond click was skipped
  by construction. It now enters at `hitStepMoveHitLoop` (Champions overrides it at
  `data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:428`) for a volley row only, reads the arrival count BACK from the
  authority rather than computing it, and hands that count to MEDICHAM as `hit.hits`. Single-hit rows
  still enter at `moveHit` and are byte-identical. `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: `dmgRange` now
  pre-stamps move ids, because `hitPlanOf` reads `mv.id` and the lazy `stampMoveIds()` did not run
  until `dmgRangeOneHit`, one call later.
- **Measured.** `0 of 6000` unchanged, and **`skipped_multihit` 134 → 0, `skipped_ability_multihit`
  17 → 0** — `data/engine-diff.json`, n=6000, seed 20260804, release `3c2b2f9ac845`, Showdown
  `20ad99ffc9a5a4a4e8fb56ab04ad8e4255b3f2b4`. **142 of those 6,000 rows are volleys**: `volley.move_rows`
  130 across 14 move ids, `volley.bond_rows` 12, arrivals x1:7 x2:66 x3:20 x4:7 x5:36 x6:6, all sixteen
  roll-index arms at 0. Predicted beforehand at 15 disagreements in a 5–40 interval
  (`data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-10-multihit.json`). Census re-run and **unmoved at 835 live /
  835 probed / 0 missing**; whole-game differential re-run on the new release and **unmoved** at
  BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 961 with 1 declared protocol divergence; `engine/quarantine.js` **GATE: OPEN**.
- **Basis.** unchanged. The new sample is a strict superset of the old one — the same question over a
  wider population, so `0` and `0` are one series.
- **Supersedes.** ~~`skipped_multihit: 134`, `skipped_ability_multihit: 17`~~ retracted — those counters
  now read 0 and the rows are compared. ~~*"tests/test-mechanics.js is now the ONLY guard"* on Parental
  Bond and on the multi-hit family~~ retracted — the damage differential is a guard on both again.
  The remaining skip is smaller and named: `volley.unstageable` 1 (Dragon Darts, the authority's own
  loop throws) and `volley.no_arrival` 4 (Sucker Punch x3, Last Resort x1 — the authority refused the
  click at `singleEvent('Try')`).
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md` and `docs/SUMMARY.md`, wherever the damage
  differential's coverage is described. **AND ONE THING IS OWED NOW, NOT AT THE MAJOR:**
  `engine/status.js:476-493` and `engine/coverage.js:647-652` print *"the volley loop has never been
  damage-compared"* unconditionally and now name all fourteen `multiHit` moves as never drawn. Both are
  MEASURE's files, so this pass FILED it (ROADMAP #575) rather than fixing it — but `--write` stamps
  that sentence into `docs/ENGINE.md`'s GENERATED block, so it is published and false.

## [6.3.0] — 2026-09-10 — CLAUDE.md described the store's 100 MB deadline in the present tense after sharding removed it

- **What changed.** Two passages in `CLAUDE.md` corrected. The file stated
  `data/games.ladder.jsonl.gz` is "51.8 MB and grows 1.12 MB/day", crossing 100,000,000 bytes around
  **2026-10-17** and taking the ingest with it, and called sharding "in flight". Sharding landed.
- **Measured.** `git ls-files` returns **nothing** for either store monolith. The tracked store is
  **30 parsed shards and 26 raw**; a routine shard is ~4 MB; the largest tracked blob under `data/` is
  **58.7 MB**, the 2026-09-09 recovery shard holding the 15,862 recovered games. A dated shard is
  written once and does not grow, so no tracked file is on a trajectory to the wall.
- **Supersedes.** ~~`data/games.ladder.jsonl.gz` is 51.8 MB and grows 1.12 MB/day, crossing 100 MB
  around 2026-10-17~~ — retracted. The 100 MB limit itself is unchanged and still binding; only the
  deadline is gone. The dated reasoning in the PDF-churn section is left standing with a pointer,
  because a dated paragraph is not rewritten in place.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Owed to the next major.** None — `CLAUDE.md` is not in the living-document set.
- **How it was found.** A session quoted the deadline to Will as live risk; he said *"i thought we
  fixed the october looming issue"*. He had. Same shape as the fourteen handoffs and the ban list of
  four, arriving through the file that names the failure. The durable lesson is in the correction:
  **ask `git ls-files`, not `ls`** — an untracked 50 MB file threatens nothing.

## [5.283.0] — 2026-09-10 — the board compares item disposition, and it found that this engine never recorded a spent item
- **What changed.** `engine/board_state.js` gains `last_item` / `ate_berry` on both body readers, wired only after its own declared `wrong_if` was STAGED and survived (`tests/probe_item_disposition.js`, 8 arms, raw fields off both engines: 4 REMOVAL arms — Knock Off vs Leftovers, Knock Off vs Sitrus, Trick, Thief — read `-/--` on both, so no removal path writes either field). The leaf immediately found a defect the row had not predicted: **medicham2 had no `Pokemon#useItem` door**, so White Herb, Mental Herb, Focus Sash and Fling spent an item and recorded nothing, and Recycle could only ever give back a berry. `recordItemUsed` in `engine/medicham2-browser.js` is that door, at six call sites (two unreachable in this regulation and kept correct). The `NOT_COMPARED` row is replaced by a comment recording both things it got wrong. ROADMAP #573 filed for the `AfterUseItem` third, not fixed. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-item-disposition-leaf.md`.
- **Measured.** THE LEAF PARTED **192 of 961** pinned-pool games with the engine unfixed and **0 of 961** with it fixed — BOARD-MATERIAL read as `state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`, `--games 1200 --turns 50 --arm middle --steering empirical --end-state --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, 961 games played, proven to be the SAME sample (identical `first_divergences` head, `coverage` block and `classes`). Stage 1 `data/verification/gd-stage1-item-disposition.json` (release `8ac9c4d888f1`), stage 2 `data/verification/gd-stage2-item-disposition.json` (release `ea3fead04c70`); every one of the 192 was `active[].last_item`, `ate_berry` contributed **zero**. Predicted in writing beforehand at 250 in a 100–450 interval (`data/verification/_prediction-2026-09-10-item-disposition.json`). PUBLISHED CHAIN, all on release `cbd510bc2b13`: `data/game-differential.json` BOARD-MATERIAL **0 of 961**, `data/engine-diff.json` 6000 compared / **0 disagreed**, roster items/abilities/moves **0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE** (142/139/487 matched), census **835 live / 0 missing** unchanged, `node engine/quarantine.js` → **GATE: OPEN**. Corner arms UNMOVED at **16 of 961** and **15 of 961** with identical first-board-divergence seed sets (`data/verification/gd-{top,bottom}-tie-first-2026-09-10.json`).
- **Basis.** unchanged — "board-material 0 of 961" answers the same question and the old and new figures link. Said explicitly because the COMPARISON widened: the earlier 0 was true in the fields then compared, and the new 0 is true in those plus two. The gate was already OPEN before this batch (`data/game-differential.json` at 07:04:15Z read 0 board-material and 1 declared protocol divergence); **nothing here opened it**.
- **Supersedes.** ~~"What differs is the item DISPOSITION: Showdown records Colbur as EATEN BY ITSELF, medicham2 as KNOCKED OFF"~~ retracted — `knock_off_roadmap_80.verdict` in `data/game-differential.json`, quoted verbatim into `board_state.js`'s `NOT_COMPARED` row as a published finding. The same artifact's own `arms` block already contradicted it (both engines emit `[eat]` then `[weaken]` on the Colbur arm) and the probe reads `colburberry/ate` on both. Corrected in `engine/game_differential.js` in this pass with the record of what it said; the 192 games came from the Focus Sash and from no berry at all.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md` (the leaf, the `useItem` door, the corrected verdict); nothing in the white paper, the deck, `SUMMARY.md` or `MODELS.md` — no figure any of them publishes moved.

## [5.282.0] — 2026-09-10 — the speed-tie corner arms now measure something, and ties are clean
- **What changed.** `engine/game_differential.js`: `--arm` moved `ARMS_RUN` and not the arm whose games become `results`, so a corner-only run played 961 games, assigned none of them, and published a bar of `0 - 0` that is byte-identical to a perfect score. `RUN_PRIMARY = ARMS_RUN[0]` now drives `results`, the `mid_void` gate, `PINS.primary` and `MODE`; `PRIMARY_ARM` stays `ARMS[0]` for the eight external callers that read it as a default pin. A run whose primary arm plays no game, compares no boundary, or whose corner pin is incomplete now sets `void: true`, blanks `diverged`/`mid_void`/`corner_pin`/`state` and exits non-zero. `mid_void` is REPLACED on a corner arm by `corner_pin`, a pin-completeness receipt — a corner arm has no dice stream to desynchronise, so a void filter there could only publish a 0 that reads as ignorance. New probe `tests/probe_corner_arm_measures.js`, red on the pre-fix bytes with two knob-cleared controls. **No engine byte moved: the release cut after the edits is `8ac9c4d888f1`, unchanged.** Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-corner-arms.md`.
- **Measured.** THE TIE QUESTION IS **CLEAN**: **71,009 speed-tie groups resolved** (`speed_ties.tied_groups_resolved` 38,319 + 32,690) across the two corners' 1,922 games, with **zero** board-material divergences attributable to tie order — `data/verification/game-differential-{top,bottom}-tie-first.json`, release `8ac9c4d888f1`, census `257acf955593`, pool `data/team-pool-frozen`, `--steering empirical --end-state --games 1200 --turns 50`, 961 games played per arm. BOARD-MATERIAL, read as `state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`: **16 of 961** top, **15 of 961** bottom. `corner_pin` reads **0** shapes or streams with more than one value on either arm, over 186,425/160,957 and 139,193/132,463 draws. THE MIDDLE ARM IS UNMOVED — 961 games, 10,705 of 10,705 boundaries identical, **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 961**, **NARRATION 0 undeclared of 961**, `mode` byte-identical (`data/verification/game-differential-middle-recheck.json`, generated 2026-09-10T08:03:18.736Z, against `data/game-differential.json` at 07:04:15.006Z); `node engine/quarantine.js` → GATE: OPEN.
- **Basis.** unchanged — the headline answers the same question it did this morning and answers it identically. What is new is that a corner arm now answers anything at all.
- **Supersedes.** ~~"the two corners share ZERO board-material causes — 14 top-only, 12 bottom-only, 0 in both, the signature of unshared dice"~~ retracted (`docs/_reports/2026-09-10-phase1-census-repin.md` §5). It was computed over artifacts that compared **no board**, in a different cause vocabulary (`end_state[0].summary.by_cause`). On the board-leaf vocabulary the corners share three shapes, the largest being `active[].hp + party.<mon>.hp` at 4 top / 3 bottom — and the "unshared dice" reading cannot apply at all, because `corner_pin` measures both engines answering every draw from a constant with zero violations. That report is a dated findings record and is not edited; the figure is retracted here.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ENGINE.md` (the corner-arm instrument and the tie verdict); nothing in the white paper, the deck, `SUMMARY.md` or `MODELS.md` — no figure any of them publishes moved.

## [5.280.0] — 2026-09-10 — the last illegal fixture is repaired, the census is re-pinned, and the gate chain re-run on it is unmoved
- **What changed.** `engine/game_differential.js` staged **Incineroar holding Knock Off** at four sites — a set the game would refuse, and the last illegal fixture in the repository (`tests/test-fixture-legality.js`, 2 FAILED). The carrier was DERIVED, never typed: `CS.moveCarriers('Knock Off')` answers **95 legal carriers** in `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`, and the three load-bearing sites move to **Pangoro** (Fighting/**Dark** so the STAB the arms are priced on survives; atk 124 against 115; spe 58 against 60, so no script's turn order moves; Iron Fist inert because Knock Off carries no `punch` flag). The fourth site is the sandstorm speed-sort row, where Knock Off is a filler no script clicks and the premise IS Incineroar's slowness — there the DECORATION was replaced with Close Combat and the body left alone. Then `data/mechanics-census.json` was regenerated after two chains of being deliberately pinned, and the whole gate chain was re-run on it. **No engine byte moved: the release id is unchanged at `8ac9c4d888f1`.** Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-phase1-census-repin.md`.
- **Measured.** Census `1da84d77888e` → **`257acf955593`**, **835 probed / 835 live / 0 missing**, **0 rows added, 0 removed, 0 status flips** — `data/mechanics-census.json`. Exactly two rows' CONTENT moved and both stay LIVE: Iron Head's flinch rate 20.3% → 19.1% over 6000 turns (unseeded noise) and `seen.terrainSparedAirborne` **+4 → +2**, the footprint of 5.279.0's terrain-gate fix. On the new census: BOARD-MATERIAL **0 of 961** (`state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`, 10,705 of 10,705 boundaries identical) and NARRATION **0 undeclared of 961** — `data/game-differential.json`, arm `middle`, `--games 1200` (961 played), `--turns 50`, empirical steering, `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, release `8ac9c4d888f1`. Damage differential **0 of 6000** (`data/engine-diff.json`, seed 20260804); roster **142 / 139 / 487** with 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` **1313 games, 0 threw**. `node engine/quarantine.js` prints **GATE: OPEN**, 9 of 9 clauses PASS. The Knock Off arms in `data/game-differential.json`'s `knock_off_roadmap_80` block move with the carrier and now read **192 / 284 / 142**, boost ratio **1.479** against an expected 1.5 (tolerance 0.05) and reduction **0.500**.
- **Basis.** unchanged — the same questions, answered on a regenerated census, with the same answers. `node engine/arms_comparable.js` answers **NOT COMPARABLE** against HEAD's artifact for two true reasons (the instrument moved, and the steering input moved), so this is reported as *the chain re-run on the new census reads the same* and NEVER as a before/after.
- **Supersedes.** ~~the Knock Off arms at 182 / 272 / 136 and a boost ratio of 1.495~~ retracted — they were measured on a body that cannot legally hold the move and stood in no living document. Nothing else is retracted. The census figure **835 live / 835 probed / 0 missing** was a claim about release `489bea0577bc`; it is now a claim about `8ac9c4d888f1` with the same value.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row. **TWO INSTRUMENT DEFECTS were found and deliberately left**, with no figure published from either: (1) `--arm top-tie-first|bottom-tie-first` alone leaves `results` empty — `PRIMARY_ARM = ARMS[0]` is always `middle` (`engine/game_differential.js:1946`, `:7199`) — so a corner-arm artifact reads `turn_boundaries_compared: 0` and the gate's own bar computes `0 − 0 = 0` over an empty population; (2) the `mid_void` low-identity exclusion is computed only when `PRIMARY_ARM.middle` (`:7825`), so a corner arm cannot separate an engine board split from a dice-stream split. The corner arms therefore do NOT answer the tie question: their board-material cause sets share **zero** causes (14 top-only, 12 bottom-only) and are dominated by miss/crit/damage-roll draws. Both artifacts are unpublished, at `data/verification/game-differential-{top,bottom}-tie-first.json`.

## [5.279.0] — 2026-09-10 — the three undeclared narration causes are closed and the MEDICHAM gate reads OPEN
- **What changed.** Three engine fixes in `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, each with a probe shown RED on the pre-fix bytes and a `MEDI_*` knob that restores the defect. (1) Shed Tail's `passstate` branch wrote a bare `-fail` on all three of its refusals where `shedtail.onTryHit` (`data/moves.ts:16166-16179`) names the move on two of them and adds `[weak]` on one — routed through the existing `mvFailNamed`, so the family has one implementation. (2) The aim/target/redirect segment was hoisted above the charge branch, where `getMoveTargets` sits in the authority (`sim/battle-actions.ts:466` against `PrepareHit` at `:591`), and the draw is gated on the authority's own `isCharging` (`sim/pokemon.ts:829-835`) so a REAL charge turn still draws nothing. (3) `priorityRefusedAbove` gained an `only` source selector: the ability half stays at `TryMove` (`:486`) and the terrain half moved below the move's own `Try` (`:590`), so a Sucker Punch the game refuses for its own reason is no longer refused by the floor. `tests/roster.js:7105`'s `blocks-priority` plant anchor was re-aimed after (3) changed the signature it string-matches. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-narration-close.md`.
- **Measured.** NARRATION **0 of 961** — clause `whole-game differential / NARRATION`, 1 narration-only raw less 1 declared (the closeted perish drain, ROADMAP #440); BOARD-MATERIAL **0 of 961**, 10,705 of 10,705 turn boundaries identical — `data/game-differential.json`, `--games 1200` (961 played), arm `middle`, empirical steering, census `1da84d77888e` (committed bytes, NOT regenerated), `--team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, `--turns 50`, release `8ac9c4d888f1`. Damage differential **0 of 6000** (`data/engine-diff.json`, seed 20260804); roster **142 / 139 / 487, 0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE, 0 dead plant anchors** (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` 1313 games, 0 threw. `node engine/quarantine.js` prints **GATE: OPEN**, 9 of 9 clauses PASS. `node engine/arms_comparable.js` answers **COMPARABLE** against the superseded arm — same steering digest, same 961 games.
- **Basis.** unchanged — 3 of 961 became 0 of 961, which is the same question answered better, and the arms were checked COMPARABLE rather than assumed. **The gate OPENING is a basis change and it is NOT declared here**: nothing downstream has been re-run, so no withheld figure has been restored and no reader can yet be told anything new. That fold-in is ROADMAP #57 and it is Will's release to declare.
- **Supersedes.** ~~NARRATION 3 of 961 across 4 causes~~ retracted — it was a claim about release `5a7bd8a8178a` and stood in no living document. The three causes that stopped are Shed Tail's `[weak]`, Lightning Rod against `-prepare`, and Sucker Punch against Psychic Terrain.
- **Owed to the next major.** `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md`, `docs/ABRA-technical-docs.md`, `docs/SUMMARY.md` and `docs/MODELS.md` all describe MEDICHAM as under quarantine; the gate opening rewrites that section rather than restamping it.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — the handoff collector was dropping more than half of every session's owed commands at the first shell comment
- **What changed.** `engine/orient.js` tracks fence state when it walks an `OWED, NOT RUN` block. It ended the block at the first markdown heading, and a `#` shell comment at column zero inside a ```bash fence matched that test — so any report that COMMENTED its commands was truncated at the first comment. Found because this session's own close report contributed ZERO commands: its OWED body was read as four lines. A `#` inside a fence is a comment, not a heading. Also added to `.claude/skills/start/SKILL.md` §7.
- **Measured.** Over the same 450 reports, before → after: **108 reports with runnable OWED commands / 339 commands → 166 / 710**; reports with an OWED heading but no command line fell 219 → 161. `node tests/test-orient.js` GREEN.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~"92 with runnable OWED commands (293 commands)"~~ and every later printed pair — the collector was under-reporting, not the reports under-writing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — two failure shapes and one source went back into the start skill, and the session's OWED is a command block the next session's map collects
- **What changed.** `.claude/skills/start/SKILL.md` §7 gains two shapes, each with its receipt: a killed agent's completion notification can carry its FIRST message rather than its last (the weekly limit killed two agents whose notifications both read "I'll start by reading the required docs" while one had 39 modified files and a finished engine fix and the other a complete 244-line report — read the TREE and `docs/_reports/`, never the notification), and a stale generated bundle HIDES untraceable figures so regenerating one can orphan a published number (`data/board-data.js`, ROADMAP #568). §8 gains the source: a probe that loads `engine/game_differential.js` REFUSES to run without `--release <id>` because requiring that module unpinned cuts a release as a side effect. `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-session-close.md` carries this session's OWED as commands under an `OWED` heading, which `engine/orient.js` collects and prints under IN FLIGHT.
- **Measured.** `node tests/test-orient.js` GREEN — the map derives every section and each fails loudly when its input goes.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none — a skill and a dated record, neither a living document.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — the residual trio is fixed at the handler list, and every probe in tests/ now asks the validator whether a body can learn a move
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` `residualOrder` sorts the bodies that HOLD a handler in the group being walked, not every active body — the authority sorts its handler list (`sim/battle.ts:445-455`), so a handler-less body could swap a tied pair here and a lower-order handler's placement could swap its partner past it. Knob `MEDI_RESIDUAL_SORTS_BODIES=1` restores the body-only sort; four `MEDFAILS` counters record when the shadow list is absent or the replacement does not fire. The header comment that claimed the swapped order was "an order the authority cannot produce" is **struck in place and corrected** rather than deleted — it was false, and it is why six batches read the trio as unexplained. Separately (#565), all 37 remaining probes in `tests/` that decided move legality by walking raw learnset data now call `champions_sim.canLearn`, the validator's own check: the raw prevo walk accepted pre-Champions source tags, which is how illegal fixtures passed their own legality gates. Records: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-residual-trio-derivation.md`, `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-raw-learnset-sweep.md`.
- **Measured.** Coordinator-run on release `5a7bd8a8178a`: `tests/probe_residual_trio_lower_order_handler.js` and `tests/probe_residual_trio_handlerless_body.js` both GREEN on current bytes and both RED at 2 of 4 cells under the knob, each naming its mechanism — the fix is proven in both directions. The sweep: 37 of 37 files converted, raw walks at ZERO in `tests/`, 36 green / 1 red before and after with both moved verdicts attributed. **No gate artifact was re-measured against these bytes** — the whole-game, damage, roster and census figures all stand on release `489bea0577bc` and `engine/status.js` withholds them as MEASURED AGAINST A DIFFERENT ENGINE until the chain below is run.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing — no published figure moves; the re-measure decides whether the narration count does.
- **Owed to the next major.** none.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — the pre-commit hook is green on a fresh clone, and 24 of 27 tracked releases had never hashed to their manifests in git
- **What changed.** Three clauses that failed only off this laptop (docs/_reports/2026-09-09-fix-rows-wave1.md, the review's "Corrections, same day"): (1) `tests/test-artifact-rerunnable.js` now asks git what it carries — a release neither on disk nor tracked is ABSENT-ON-THIS-MACHINE (counted, not failed, not ratcheted); a tracked or on-disk release that will not open stays STRANDED; and a NEW clause hashes the COMMITTED blobs of every tracked release against its manifest (`git cat-file --batch`), which is what a clone gets — it found 24 of 27 tracked releases normalised to LF when force-added before the `data/releases/** -text` rule, renormalised in `0ec9c45a`; (2) `engine/artifact_audit.js` drops the bundle gate's MTIME clause (checks B and G already judge the pair by content; fresh-checkout mtimes are arbitrary and this repository's own rule says mtime is no evidence); (3) `build/build_archive_index.js` strips CR before its header regex — a CRLF clone rebuilt the index with 0 of 16 headers parsed. Deliberate break on release `7d66b526659e`: both new clauses red by name, then restored. Record: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-fresh-clone-hook.md`. Also landed: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-600-medicham-doc-pass-draft.md` — the 6.0.0 MEDICHAM fold-in drafted OLD → NEW with ~150 `path:field` citations, every batch-movable figure marked RE-READ; no living document touched.
- **Measured.** Clean sibling worktree of `origin/main`: before, `tests/test-artifact-rerunnable.js` 88 STRANDED and the hook BLOCKED; after, 78 absent / 0 stranded / 6 of 6 clauses green, bundle gate 0 GAP on identical bytes, `tests/test-docs-current.js` 35/0, the whole hook green in 9.6 s. ROADMAP #554 closes on this.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none.

Measured on 2026-09-10: the `:07` collector fired at 00:59 (52 min late) and not at all for the 01:07 and 02:07 slots; the six-hourly ingest's 00:17 slot never fired. ## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — the residual trio is one mechanism, derived and not yet fixed: `residualOrder` sorts bodies where the authority sorts handlers, and the record's "65/75, not a tie" was a base-Speed read
- **What changed.** No engine edit. Two probes, `tests/probe_residual_trio_lower_order_handler.js` and `tests/probe_residual_trio_handlerless_body.js`, each RED 2 of 4 on release `7d66b526659e` with green control cells, one per swap variant. The mechanism, read on the pinned authority checkout: `residualOrder` (`engine/medicham2-browser.js`) sorts every active body, fainted included, group by group; the authority sorts one flat list of residual HANDLERS (`sim/battle.ts:492-507`, `speedSort` `:429-458`). Under the differential's pin no tie die is drawn on either side (`engine/game_differential.js:1859` `o.tie = () => 0`), so a tied pair's order is whatever each selection sort's swap history leaves — and the two lists differ when a tied body also carries a lower-order handler (Leftovers placed before brn), or when a faster body with no residual handler shares the field (Overqwil at 127 is not in the authority's list; medicham's body sort selects it first and its swap carries Scovillain ahead of Umbreon). Fix site `residualOrder`; the fix is the next batch, not this row. ROADMAP #563. Account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-residual-trio-derivation.md`.
- **Measured.** All three pairs are exact ties in BUILT Speed — 90/90, 117/117, 80/80 — read off the authority's `storedStats.spe` and off `freshBodies(buildPair(sheet))`: `buildPair` assigns SP by slot index (+32 / +22 / +11 / 0), so Umbreon (base 65, slot 0) and Scovillain (base 75, slot 1) are both 117. The trio is 3 of the 7 narration causes on release `489bea0577bc` (`data/game-differential.json`, `first_divergences`: `omit-protect …2660452545` t7 brn, `omit-spread …2636020596` t4 psn, `pair-protect-bust …2659228530` t7 leftovers). Narration on every measured game; board-material only in a mutual residual KO, which is unmeasured.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~"70/70, 60/60, 65/75 — two ties and one that is NOT, so not one mechanism"~~ retracted — it stood in batch S's hand list (`docs/ENGINE.md`) and in `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-session-close.md`, both dated evidence, and is corrected here by the 117/117 receipt rather than edited there. The header above `residualOrder` claims a per-group order "the authority cannot produce"; game 1 IS that order under the pin, and under real dice `speedSort` shuffles per tied group — the prose is wrong, the per-phase key is fine.
- **Owed to the next major.** none — no published figure; the fix and its re-measure (expect narration 6 → 3 of 961, board-material unchanged) land in this release.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — fixture legality, batch 2: 24 of 25 illegal sets repaired with format-derived carriers, and the probes' own legality gates had passed them because a raw learnset walk accepted gen-7 source tags
- **What changed.** 24 probe and test files repaired with carriers derived from `gen9championsvgc2026regmb` at Showdown `20ad99f` — Staraptor Final Gambit, Umbreon Toxic and Curse, Hydreigon Roar + Dragon Tail, Gourgeist-Small Explosion, Scizor Knock Off, Audino Simple Beam, Chandelure Power Split, Lucario Swords Dance, Garganacl and Vanilluxe Explosion, Slowking as the one Substitute-probe mover, Snorlax Curse, Skarmory Whirlwind, Toucannon U-turn; six over-four-move sets split per arm; the Nasty Plot filler's three illegal bodies swapped or re-fillered. Root cause in the instrument, not the engine: thirteen probes' "LEGALITY, DERIVED AND REFUSED" gates walked `dex.data.Learnsets` up the prevo chain and returned true on ANY entry whatever its source tag (Clefairy `["7V"]` for Clefable Curse), where `TeamValidator#checkCanLearn` says no — those 13 now ask `champions_sim.canLearn`. Two premises could not be kept and the arms say so: no Infiltrator Simple Beam carrier exists (Audino is its only carrier), and Incineroar has no legal damaging pivot (the U-turn arm runs on Toucannon). Three regressions of the batch's own — literal `Milotic` in two probes' judgement regexes, a slot-1 body given a filler the request never offers — were caught by HEAD-fixture controls and fixed. ROADMAP #565. Account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-fixture-legality-batch2.md`.
- **Measured.** `node tests/test-fixture-legality.js`, re-run at this pass: NEW illegal sets 25 → **1**, NEW declarations 19 → **1** (632 files, 2367 set declarations, 753 distinct sets); the survivor is `engine/game_differential.js:6186,6256,6325,6348` — `incineroar @ (no item) / Blaze / [Knock Off, Protect]`, the owner's file, whose damage-interior arms compare a Knock Off figure that must be re-derived for the replacement body. 24 of 25 touched probes green; `tests/probe_default_target_side.js` RED 12/12 on a `near-side draws counted` expectation that is red on the HEAD fixture and on release `7d66b526659e` too — pre-existing, ENGINE's counter or the probe's expectation. Raw-learnset walk still carried: the report counted 34 as 47 − 13; `grep -l "e.learnset && e.learnset\[id\]" tests/*.js` reads **37** at this pass, none calling `canLearn` (36 at HEAD plus `tests/probe_substitute_family.js`, new tonight).
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing published — fixtures are not a figure.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. Open and named: the survivor (#565), the 37 files (#565), the derived-cast `'Nasty Plot'` fillers outside the sweep's population, and the `probe_default_target_side` counter card.

## [5.278.0] — 2026-09-10 — narration batch Z: the substitute family is closed with a step, not the loop-nesting job the record planned; NARRATION 9 → 6 of 961, BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 961 with 0 void
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`: the doll is absorbed at step 0 of `spreadMoveHit` over every row (`_stepSubAbsorb`, moved out of `_stepApply`), the step-1 writes `_stepDamage` used to make inline are deferred to `_stepPriceLines` below it (the DECISIONS — which berry, whether the shield was pierced — stay at the price; only the WRITES move), and each doll arrival of a volley writes its own effectiveness/crit line; knob `MEDI_SUB_ABSORB_AT_APPLY=1` restores the old order and stamps `MEDFAILS.subAbsorbAtApplyRestored`. **The record's loop-nesting plan is REFUTED, not deferred**: batches X and Y planned to make the arrival loop the outer loop, which needs a click both multi-arrival and multi-row, and none exists — 14 legal multi-hit moves, every one `target: normal` (Dragon Darts is `smartTarget` and already row-major), and Parental Bond refuses `move.spreadHit` (`data/abilities.ts` `parentalbond.onPrepareHit`, no Champions override). Both facts are derived on every run of `tests/probe_substitute_family.js`, which exits NOT RUN the day either stops holding. **The receipt is that probe, not the resolution-order arm the brief named**: `tests/test-resolution-order.js` has no KNOWN-OPEN arm — `a1-multihit-frequency` was promoted to `red` on 2026-08-30 and already read RED PROVEN; the file is 26/26 before and after. Probe: 5 arms RED on `7d66b526659e`, green live, each PARTING again under the knob at the card's own line; 2 controls held. Account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-10-narration-batch-Z-substitute.md`.
- **Measured.** `data/game-differential.json`, release `489bea0577bc`, generated 2026-09-10T02:18Z, pins `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --state --games 1200 --turns 50 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --census data/verification/census-pin-1da84d77888e.json`: `state.games` 961, `state.games_board_never_diverged` 961, `state.games_void_excluded` 0, `mid_void.diverged_among_usable` 7 → BOARD-MATERIAL **0 of 961**, NARRATION **6 of 961** after the one closeted row, **7 causes** (`first_divergences`, 7 of 7, uncapped at this size). Three of the four cards' games stopped, none started, one CHANGED CAUSE — it played past the substitute line and parted two lines later on a Shed Tail `[weak]` `-fail` this engine writes bare (`data/moves.ts:16166-16177`; #564). `data/engine-diff.json` 6000 compared / 6000 agreed / 0; `data/roster.items.json` 142 tested / 0 differ, `data/roster.abilities.json` 139 / 0, `data/roster.moves.json` 487 / 0; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` 1313 games / 0 threw, field for field; census 835 probed / 835 live / 0 missing (`data/mechanics-census.json`). Gate 8 of 9; narration is the only red.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~NARRATION 9 of 961, 10 causes~~ → 6 of 961, 7 causes; release ~~`7d66b526659e`~~ → `489bea0577bc` for every gate artifact.

## [5.277.0] — 2026-09-09 — the roster's scope is decided by legal carrier, once, for all three stages
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js` derives every stage's scope through one resolver, `legalCarriers(kind, e)`, before any shape rule runs: 347 legal species (`exists && !isNonstandard && tier !== 'Illegal'`, 72 mega and 83 battle-only formes among them); an ability by any slot, a move by the learnset walked up the prevo chain and across to the base forme as `learnsMove` does, an item by its mega-stone base, its `itemUser`, or anybody. Zero carriers → `out_of_scope: 'no-legal-carrier'`, never staged, dropped from `results`, counted in `scope.out_of_scope` beside `scope.carrier_derivation`. Imprison and Memento THREW were both the harness — a self-KO clicked twice; a move-sealing self volatile leaving the foes only the inert click — and both read FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH. `tests/test-stadium-roster.js` 3 failures → ALL PASS, test-side only. Account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-roster-scope-by-carrier.md`; register #555 (closed).
- **Measured.** Before → after on release `b0f5c159c46e`, `--reds --write`, one stage at a time: abilities out of scope 114 → 115, in scope 202 → 201, could-not-stage 44 → 43; moves out of scope 0 → 2, in scope 500 → 498, could-not-stage 10 → 8; items unmoved. Now `data/roster.abilities.json:scope` reads out_of_scope 115, in_scope 201, tested 139, could_not_stage_in_scope 43, unattributable 14, deferred 5, differ 0, silent 0; `data/roster.moves.json:scope` reads out_of_scope 2, in_scope 498, tested 487, could_not_stage_in_scope 8, deferred 3, differ 0; `data/roster.items.json:scope` reads in_scope 148, tested 142, could_not_stage_in_scope 6, differ 0. **The honest reading:** Will's ruling that the untested abilities are not in the game holds for 115, and 114 of those were already out; what the SCOPE line still prints — 43 fixture gaps, 14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET, 5 DEFERRED-BY-OWNER — all have legal carriers and are the deprioritised lab tail (Will, 2026-08-23), carried, not removed.
- **Basis.** unchanged.

## [5.277.0] — 2026-09-09 — a raw shard had no cap, and the first full-archive run wrote one of 56 MB
- **What changed.** `build/compress-stores.js --raw` now caps a raw shard at `SHARD_BYTES` (32 MiB of source — the parsed writer's constant, hoisted so both writers read one number) and splits a run into `-00`, `-01`, …; before, one run wrote one shard of any size, and `SHARD_BYTES` was declared below the `--raw` block in its temporal dead zone. Shown red on a scratch copy (HEAD: 1 shard from 36.6 MiB of source) then green (2 shards, 32.00 and 4.61 MiB; `--restore-raw` from the shards alone sha256-identical to the original). Real tree under `tools/lownode.cmd`: 0 new shards, every raw store up to date. `engine/gate_fail_and_silent.js`: `PIN` re-seeded 30 → 1 on the reproducible pinned sample (census `098de5770623`, pool `0d103fb9fa87`, 961 games) — the old pin named a live-pool sample of 2026-08-18 that no run could reproduce, so exit 3 REGRESSION was unreachable; verdict unchanged at LIVE, exit 1, one cause (a Trick `-fail` this engine never announces, #559). `--restore-parsed` reconciled the local ladder store 92,379 → 92,431 rows; `tests/test-workflow-paths.js` 5/1 → 6/0. Account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-raw-shard-cap.md`; register #556 (closed), #558 (open, OPS).
- **Measured.** `data/raw/games.ladder/20260909T2052-00.jsonl.gz` (commit `71771f0b`) is 58,753,177 B gzipped from 411,333,183 B of source, 76,741 logs — 56.03 MiB, over GitHub's 50 MB warning and under the 100 MB wall; it is pushed and permanent, because history is never rewritten. Growth budget at 176 games/h: one shard per hourly run, about 135 KB each, 24 a day; a shard reaches the cap only after 6,260 logs in one run; worst case with zero compression 33.6 MB. Observed, not acted on: the ladder parsed store holds 92,431 rows and the raw shards 84,832 logs — 7,599 parsed rows have no raw log.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure moves.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. Open and named: two store rows carry U+FFFD in the `|win|` line (#558); 7,599 ladder rows without a raw log, owed a register row and a count of ids; the first CI pass through the 56 MB shard on the default heap.

## [5.276.0] — 2026-09-09 — Reg M-C went live on Showdown and the detector said it did not exist, because it trusted the client bundle over the replay server
- **What changed.** `engine/next_regulation.js` gained a replay-search arm: it derives candidate ids by advancing the active regulation's letter (cap 3) and probes `replay.pokemonshowdown.com/search.json?format=<id>`; a format the replay server is serving is a format the server accepted, whatever `play.pokemonshowdown.com/data/formats.js` says. After: `gen9championsvgc2026regmc` and `gen9championsvgc2026regmcbo3` read `candidate [replay]`. `tests/test-next-regulation.js` +6 checks, GREEN 25. Reg M-B stays `active`; nothing is simulated, only collected — Will, 2026-09-09: *"focus on getting reg mb perfect before looking at the new ones, i just want to start building up the store."* Record: `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-regmc-scraper-test.md`.

Page-1 upload rate at 18:35Z: bo1 ≈ 111 games/h, bo3 ≈ 42 games/h against a rolling ~1,250-replay search pool — the reason the hourly collector and the gap detector are in flight in the same session. Reg M-C carries 29 species keys, 10 moves and 17 items the Reg M-B dex marks `Past`/`Future`; noted for the rotation, not acted on.

- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none — collection, not a published figure.

- **Supersedes.** ~~0.7048, accuracy 0.502~~ retracted (WAR on the clean store, the v3.2.0 run — stood in the white paper, MODELS, SUMMARY, ROLE-FAMILY and PUBLICATION and has been DELETED there); ~~"carries a small real signal" / "barely clears" / "only edges" a coin / "beats the rating baseline (0.6905)"~~ retracted; ~~0.53~~ retracted (NMF error) and ~~"topic coherence is the next refinement"~~ deleted; ~~1,205~~ retracted (CHOMP-EV games), ~~0.6918~~, ~~0.512 [0.493, 0.535]~~, ~~0.505~~ retracted, and the selection audit's four turn/rating figures with "making the null conservative" retracted — the audit measured zero excluded games (the two rating literals are written in CHANGELOG only; struck here they would register and accuse `docs/ENGINE.md:35712`); ~~35.9% (CI 35.2–36.5), 71.6%, 2.27 nats, 4.54, 2.91~~ retracted (XATU clone — in no artifact); ~~0.687 vs 0.693~~ retracted and the bot-filtered "higher-rated player wins" rate with its CI [49.9, 54.9] retracted (§1 ceiling — no artifact; the percent is written in CHANGELOG only, because struck here it would register and accuse `docs/MEASURE.md:6271-6272, 6342`, an unrelated majority-class baseline); ~~"about one in six" / "3 of 19"~~ retracted → 2 of 19 (5.273.0; ENGINE.md's own copies now struck); ~~"the union driver is the confirmed cause"~~, ~~"duplicates from `merge -X ours`"~~, ~~"an unattended auto-commit publishes on a timer here"~~ retracted as mechanisms.

## [5.275.0] — 2026-09-09 — the sharding cutover dropped 11,110 ladder and 4,752 bo3 games from the tracked store; recovered from history, and `--check` now reads what git tracked
- **What changed.** Five write-once shards under `data/parsed/` carry every id that `d2a418a5` (2026-09-06 15:34Z, the last tracked monoliths) held and the cutover `18432bcb` did not: `games.ladder/20260909T1708-{00,01,02}` (11,110 rows, 66.5 MB of source) and `games.bo3/20260909T1708-{00,01}` (4,752 rows, 44.3 MB). No existing shard changed. ots lost nothing. `build/compress-stores.js --check` now also asserts the shards carry every id tracked at `HEAD~1`, in either tracked form, and `engine/dedupe_store.py` writes LF on Windows (`newline='\n'`) — it wrote CRLF, measured on a 5-row probe. Report: `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-fix-store-recovery.md`.
- **Measured.** Tracked ids ladder 81,269 -> 92,379, bo3 28,049 -> 32,801, ots 4,167. `comm -23` of d2a418a5's ids against all shards: 0 / 0 / 0. `--verify-parsed` IDENTICAL: `cde0fa05d5177469` (ladder, 92,379 rows, 481,072,929 B), `072c4c61ed23bea3` (bo3, 32,801 rows, 298,313,305 B), `cd21077a4578afa3` (ots, unchanged). The new `--check` clause: green on the tree in 8.7 s; red on a one-row break; red on a worktree at `18432bcb` (`LOST 11513` / `LOST 5764`). `engine/sanity_check.py` 95/1 -> 94/2: the added red is `brought` = 6 on `gen9championsvgc2026regmb-2676161109`, a record d2a418a5 already tracked.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** The 2026-09-06 sharding row's "Supersedes. Nothing. No published figure changed value." That row verified the shards against the live local file (`IDENTICAL 12 shard(s) -> 76833 rows`) and not against the tracked `.gz` they replaced; the tracked corpus shrank by 11,110 ladder and 4,752 bo3 games at that commit and no check said so. Every count published off the tracked store between `18432bcb` and this row is short by up to that many games.
