## [5.274.0] — 2026-09-09 — batch X: an immunity priced as a zero chance still drew a die, and the substitute question turns out not to be about the step list
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, three mechanisms, each shown RED first with a restoring knob. (1) **`hitStepAccuracy` `continue`s ABOVE its own `randomChance`**; this engine priced a type immunity as `hitChance -> 0`, and **zero is finite**, so it drew and missed — two defects in one return value, the wrong line AND an accuracy draw the authority never takes. (2) **`addVolatile` returns false on a condition with no `onRestart`**, and the redirect branch wrote both the mark and the line unconditionally; `attrStill()` is part of the fix rather than decoration. (3) **A charge move's pre-major line is owed at `onBeforeMove` priority 100**, the maximum of fifteen, and was written at the `|move|` line, which a refused body never reaches — **the engine's own comment had named this hole on 2026-08-24**.
- **Measured.** **Gate narration 15 → 12 of 961, causes 16 → 13, zero transfers.** **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 after every one of the three edits, on three separate releases — not once at the end.** The cards' own games: **3 stopped, 0 started, 0 changed cause**. Census 830/830; roster 0 DIFFER / 0 DID-NOT-FIRE with anchors 18/44/36/17 live and **none dead**; `test-engine-diff` 6000/0; `test-resolution-order` still 26/26; `probe_red_demo` 200/0/0. Gate 1 of 9.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. The narration figure moves under an unchanged basis, which is a MINOR.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **THE SUBSTITUTE FAMILY HAS A PLAN FOR THE FIRST TIME, AND IT IS NOT A STEP-LIST PROBLEM.** A step-0 doll slot is constructible and probably costs no dice, but it fixes **arrival 1 only**: this engine's arrival loop lives INSIDE `_stepApply` while the authority's is OUTSIDE `spreadMoveHit`, so arrivals 2..n would need a second implementation of the doll rule. The correct change is to make the arrival loop the **outer** loop over the `_stepDamage` … `_stepAfterHitField` segment — the same segment already looked up dynamically for `smartTarget`. One batch, one named trap (`_stepUpdate`), and `test-resolution-order`'s declared KNOWN-OPEN arm closes as the receipt. **Two standing derivations in the engine's own comments are WRONG and are now named:** `DamagingHit` sorts by `compareLeftToRightOrder` (order, priority, target INDEX — deterministic) and not `speedSort`, **so the dice argument that deferred the Spicy Spray fix rests on a false premise**; and `electromorphosis`, an order-1 member, sits in `_stepBuffOnHit`. Six causes are left with their derivation rather than a guess, including the post-KO switch order, whose authority sort key is the **fainted** body's Speed and not the arriving one's.

## [5.273.0] — 2026-09-09 — batch W: a red test returns to green with all six anchors re-aimed, a bounced move draws at the address of the body it came back at, and Instruct is refused when the slot it would repeat is empty
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` and `tests/test-resolution-order.js`; three fixes, two new probes, two new restore knobs. **W1 — the red test.** `tests/test-resolution-order.js` was FAILING at 10 of 26 arms. Six were stale PLANT anchors — `buff-above-secondaries` x3, `hitcount-in-the-packet-loop` x2, `react-batched` x1 — each of which had named a NEIGHBOUR it did not care about, and the neighbour moved (`_stepThawDamagingHit` inserted between two names by batch Q2; a condition that grew a Parental Bond sibling on 2026-09-07; a nine-line block one of whose lines became `_reactAddr(()=>_damagingHit(1))` on 2026-09-06). **All six were re-aimed onto the smallest unique string that IS the fix and all six read CAUGHT; none was retired.** The other four were `residualUpdate*` counter mismatches, and they were the ENGINE moving while the COUNTER did not follow: a correct third caller of `residualUpdatePass` — the replacement switch's own `eachEvent('Update')`, `sim/battle.ts:2858`, added 2026-09-06 — folded itself into two counters whose declared nouns say *"1 with no weather up"* and *"exactly one per residual"*. `RESIDUAL_GI_REFILL` now excludes it from the five residual-phase counters; the refill keeps `refillUpdatePasses`/`refillUpdateAte`, still asserted by `tests/probe_refill_update_pass.js`. Four `++` sites, no resolution moves. **W2 — the bounced move.** Magic Bounce answers at `TryHit`, step 2 of eight, while `hitStepAccuracy` is step 5, so the ORIGINAL click never reaches a die; `this.actions.useMove(newMove, target, {target: source})` (`data/abilities.ts:2433-2436`) is a whole move whose `activeTarget` is the body it came back at, and it takes the one draw of the pair. The count was already right here and the ADDRESS was not — `MID_TGT` is written once per action from the target the CLICK named, so we drew at the BOUNCER's slot. The status branch now moves the address, asks `hitChance` with the bouncer as attacker, and names the bouncer on `-miss` (without that third part the address fix would replace one divergence with another). New counter `MEDSEEN.bounceAddressReaimed`; probe `tests/probe_bounce_accuracy_address.js`, knob `MEDI_BOUNCE_KEEPS_SOURCE=1`. **W3 — Instruct.** `(moveSlot && moveSlot.pp <= 0)` is the last clause of Instruct's `onHit` and this branch never had it, so the repeat was queued and then refused one step later with `|cant|…|nopp|…`, two lines the authority does not write. `ppLeft(t,_mid) === 0` — not `!ppLeft(...)`, because a null is the authority's falsy `moveSlot` and must not refuse. New counter `MEDSEEN.instructRefusedByLastMovePP`; probe `tests/probe_instruct_lastmove_pp.js`, knob `MEDI_INSTRUCT_NO_PP_REFUSAL=1`.
- **Measured.** **`tests/test-resolution-order.js` 10 failing → 0 of 26 arms, exit 0.** **NARRATION-ONLY 18 → 16 causes / 18 → 16 games; gate NARRATION 17 → 15 of 961; ZERO TRANSFERS** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `7489a6cc064d`, n=961 games at `--turns 50`, flags `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --write`, arms `de38d17e15a2`, against baseline release `f6ecf4222048` measured under the identical six flags. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 before, after, and after every edit** (`state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`). Both cards' own games shown fixed by diffing a `--dump-games 60` pass on each release keyed on `config + seed`: **2 games stopped diverging, 0 started, 0 changed cause.** `data/mechanics-census.json` **830 live / 0 missing / 830 probed / 0 threw / 0 hollow**; all four roster stages **0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE, anchors 18/44/36/17 all live**; `data/engine-diff.json` **6000 compared, 0 disagreed**; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` **1313 games, 0 threw**; `tests/probe_red_demo.js` **200 demonstrations, 0 hollow, 0 unappliable**. `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses failing** — NARRATION and nothing else.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~NARRATION-ONLY 18 causes / 18 games and gate narration 17 of 961~~ retracted — batch V's figures on release `f6ecf4222048`, superseded by 16/16 and 15 of 961 on `7489a6cc064d`. They stand as history in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch V block, which states its own release. ~~the authority starts `perish1` on both foes where this engine starts it on one, on the Instruct card~~ **retracted** — it was the ten-line `--dump-games` window (`engine/game_differential.js`), not the game: our two extra lines pushed `|-start|p2a: Oranguru|perish1` to line eleven. That figure appeared only in the batch V row above and in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch V hand list, both of which state the bound it fell foul of; no living document carries it. **With it goes the count of board defects hiding under a narration label: ~~3 of 19 causes, about one in six~~ retracted, the measured figure is 2 of 19** — the multi-hit volley (batch V) and the bounced Sleep Powder (this row), both now fixed.
- **Owed to the next major.** The standing narration count, and two named gaps left open at the bounce site and written into the engine comment there: **a bounced move's STATUS is still applied with the CLICKER as its source**, so Safeguard and Synchronize attribute it to the wrong body, and **`newMove.pranksterBoosted = false` is not modelled**, so a Prankster clicker's bounced move is still refused by a Dark type. Neither is exercised by the pool. The four-card substitute family stands unchanged on batch V's step-boundary diagnosis, and the residual trio (`brn<>brn`, `psn<>psn`, `leftovers<>leftovers`) was skipped deliberately for the sixth batch running.

## [5.272.0] — 2026-09-09 — batch V: a multi-hit volley no longer stops at the doll it breaks, and the count of board defects hiding under a narration label
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, plus re-aimed source anchors in `tests/probe_red_demo.js`. **The substitute branch inside `_stepApply` ended `R.out = true; return;` for the WHOLE click**, so a two-hit move that broke a doll on arrival 1 never threw arrival 2 at the body behind it. The authority remakes its target list every arrival — `} else { targetsCopy = targets.slice(0); }` (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:459-473`) — and writes `targets[i] = null` into that COPY (`:351-354`), so `HIT_SUBSTITUTE` drops the row for one arrival and nothing more; none of the three loop guards stops the volley either. The branch now walks the arrivals: the clamp is per arrival against the doll's HP at that moment (`data/moves.ts:18341-18344`), the doll's `-activate|[damage]` / `-end` is written per arrival, the arrivals are SKIPPED and not SLICED (`R.pkFrom`, because `R.pkIdx[i]`, `R.crits[i]` and `R.reprice(crit, i)` are keyed on the ORIGINAL arrival number), and `_dollPaid` is an OFFSET inside `_reDealt` so Endure, the Focus Sash and the arrival re-price keep speaking about the body's half alone. **Second line, same root:** `-hitcount` fires on `move.multihit` alone below the loop (`scripts.ts:547-551`), so it is announced even over a row a doll dropped — `_stepHitCount.runsWhenOut`, a flag on the step rather than a flush below the driver, because that line's position against `_stepFaint` and `_stepHpThresholdBoost` is the whole of two earlier wires. New probe `tests/probe_multihit_through_doll.js`, restore knob `MEDI_VOLLEY_STOPS_AT_DOLL=1`.
- **Measured.** **NARRATION-ONLY 19 → 18 causes / 19 → 18 games; gate NARRATION 18 → 17 of 961; ZERO TRANSFERS** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `f6ecf4222048`, n=961 games at `--turns 50`, flags `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --write`, pool digest `0d103fb9fa87`, arms `de38d17e15a2`, against baseline release `7c6716f23df6` measured under the identical six flags. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 before, after, and after every edit** (`state.games` less `state.games_board_never_diverged`). The card's own game is shown fixed by diffing a `--dump-games 60` pass on each release, keyed on `config + seed`: **1 game stopped diverging, 0 started, 0 changed cause.** `data/mechanics-census.json` **830 live / 0 missing / 830 probed / 0 threw / 0 hollow**; all four roster stages **0 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE, anchors 18/44/36/17 all live**; `data/engine-diff.json` **6000 agreed, 0 disagreed**; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` **1313 games, 0 threw**; `tests/probe_red_demo.js` **200 demonstrations, 0 hollow, 0 unappliable**. `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses failing** — NARRATION and nothing else.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~NARRATION-ONLY 19 causes / 19 games and gate narration 18 of 961~~ retracted — batch U's figures on release `7c6716f23df6`, superseded by 18/18 and 17 of 961 on `f6ecf4222048`. They stand as history in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch U block, which states its own release. No living document carries them as current.
- **Owed to the next major.** The standing narration count, and **one sentence the headline figure needs.** `BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958` means no board difference SURVIVED to a compared turn boundary, not that none occurred — measured here rather than asserted. Over every NARRATION-ONLY game's divergence window, comparing the MULTISET of state-bearing lines (an `ordering` divergence is a permutation and reconverges; a line one engine writes and the other does not is a state one engine has and the other does not), **16 of 18 are permutations and 2 differ** — which with the one closed in this row is **3 of the 19 causes this batch started with, about one in six.** Bound: the window is the 10 lines the dump keeps (`engine/game_differential.js:4391`). The two survivors are the bounced Sleep Powder (the authority's reflected copy misses, ours lands — a body asleep here and awake there) and Instruct's refusal (ours executes it, and downstream the authority starts `perish1` on both foes where ours starts it on one); both are invisible only because the differing body stopped mattering before a boundary was sampled, and neither perish nor sleep is in `end_state_not_compared`. **ALSO OPEN AND NAMED:** the one-root hypothesis for the four-card substitute family is **REFUTED** — same line, different axis (per-arrival inside a row versus per-step across rows, the authority absorbing the doll at `spreadMoveHit` step 0 where this engine absorbs it at step 2), and the cheap emission-only fix cannot work because no insertion point in the current step list sits below the doll row's own effectiveness line and above every other row's. `tests/test-resolution-order.js` is RED at 10 of 26 arms and was demonstrated RED on the pre-batch engine under the restore knob: six stale PLANT anchors and four `residualUpdate*` counter mismatches, neither caused nor fixed here.

## [5.271.0] — 2026-09-09 — narration batch U: a smart-target move refuses an immunity in silence, Quick Claw fires above bracket 0, and hazards bite in the order they were laid
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, plus one corrected assertion in `tests/test-mechanics.js`; three mechanisms, three new probes, three restore knobs. **U1** — `hitStepTypeImmunity` asks `targets[i].runImmunity(move, !move.smartTarget)` (`sim/battle-actions.ts:661`) and `runImmunity` opens with `if (!message) return false`, so a Dragon Darts that meets an immune body writes NOTHING; this engine wrote the line every time. It is the same one-shot that already silences the shield line, so `_smartShieldSpent` is renamed `_smartSpent` and read by both roads. `tests/probe_smart_target_immune_line.js`, knob `MEDI_SMART_IMMUNE_LINE=1`. **U2** — Quick Claw's `priority <= 0` is the event's RELAY VAR, not the move's bracket (`runEvent('FractionalPriority', ..., action.move, 0)`, `sim/battle-queue.ts:249`; `action.priority = priority + action.fractionalPriority`, `sim/battle.ts:2644`), so the claw rolls, announces and nudges on a PRIORITY click too; this engine refused it above bracket 0 and threw away a roll it had already taken. `tests/probe_quick_claw_above_bracket_zero.js`, knob `MEDI_FRACPRI_PRIORITY_GATE=1`. **U3** — entry hazards tie on speed, priority and subOrder, so `resolvePriority` stamps `effectOrder` on `SwitchIn` handlers and `comparePriority` sorts it ASCENDING (`sim/battle.ts:994-999`, `:407-412`): they bite in the order they were LAID, and this engine ran four `if` blocks in a fixed source order. `tests/probe_hazard_lay_order.js`, knob `MEDI_HAZARD_FIXED_ORDER=1`.
- **Measured.** **NARRATION-ONLY 21 → 19 causes / 21 → 19 games; gate NARRATION 20 → 18 of 961** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `7c6716f23df6`, n=961 games at `--turns 50`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen`, census 830 rows, pool digest `0d103fb9fa87`, arms `de38d17e15a2`, against baseline release `2a90ecca8005`. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 after every measurement and after every edit.** U1+U2 closed two causes with **zero transfers**; U3 closed one and **one transferred within the same game**, measured by diffing a `--dump-games` pass against the baseline dump on `config + seed` — **2 games stopped diverging, 0 started, 1 changed cause**. `data/mechanics-census.json` **830 live / 0 missing / 830 probed**; all four roster stages **0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE, anchors 18/44/36/17 all live** on both releases; `data/engine-diff.json` **6000 agreed, 0 disagreed**; `data/all-mechanics-fire.json` **1313 games, 0 threw**. `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses failing** — NARRATION and nothing else.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~NARRATION-ONLY 21 causes / 21 games and gate narration 20 of 961~~ retracted — those were batch T's figures on release `eb46032d332c`, re-measured here on `2a90ecca8005` after `data/abra-tags.js` was rebuilt, and superseded by 19/19 and 18 of 961 on `7c6716f23df6`. They stand as history in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch T block, which states its own release. No living document carries them as current.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row beyond the standing narration count. **OPEN AND NAMED:** a census probe, `item/fractionalPriorityAnnounce`, was ASSERTING one of the bugs — it claimed a Quick Claw stays silent on a priority click, was green for as long as the engine agreed, and went MISSING the moment the engine was corrected; settled against a measured authority stream, not a re-reading. The substitute family is now FOUR cards and is still deliberately unattempted. `|-damage|p1a|H/H <> |move|p1a|psyshock` is **a board defect wearing a NARRATION-ONLY label** — a two-hit volley that stops when it breaks a substitute, worth 79 HP, invisible only because the body died in the same turn in both engines. Trick's missing `-fail` is newly diagnosed and blocked on a mega-stone guard that is coarser than the authority's. The residual trio was skipped entirely and deliberately, for the fourth batch running.

## [5.271.0] — 2026-09-09 — narration batch T: the Safeguard board defect closed, the shield hoisted above the ability at ten more sites, and a corpse is on the field until its faint line is written
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` and `engine/tag_dex.js`. **T1** — `safeguard.condition.onTryAddVolatile` names `yawn` explicitly and refuses the DROWSE; this engine landed it and slept the body two turns later, which is a BOARD defect batch S found and left. Fixed through the shared `sideBuffRefuses`, with the volatile ids now DERIVED onto the tag (`blocksVolatileIds`, one row moved, one field gained) and the handler's `infiltrates` exemption modelled after a probe assertion refuted the comment that had declared it unreachable (**Meowstic and Meowstic-F** are legal Infiltrator carriers that learn Yawn). **T2** — ten more `tryHitRefusal` call sites asked the ABILITY above the SHIELD, against `findPokemonEventHandlers` gathering volatiles before abilities; plus two branches with no shield check at all (`lockon`, and the unmodelled-click road) and one spurious `-fail` on the `healdesc` shield road. **T3** — `isActive` is cleared inside `faintMessages`, so a corpse is still ACTIVE on the update pass; `sourceOffField` answered "gone" at the HP transition and put Syrup Bomb's `-end` above the `|faint|`. Three new probes, six knobs, every one shown RED on the pre-fix bytes.

- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row beyond the standing narration count. **OPEN AND NAMED:** the substitute trio is now diagnosed as ONE mechanism (this engine defers a doll's `-activate|[damage]` / `-end` until after the other target's damage) and is still not attempted because moving where the absorption RESOLVES can part a board; the redirect-vs-`-prepare` order and the post-KO switch-in order are diagnosed and not attempted; the residual trio is untouched and unexplained for the third batch running; and `kind === 'boostally'` has a shield that suppresses the boost while saying nothing at all.

- **What changed.** New `engine/major_readiness.js` — it answers *would 6.0.0 be honest today, and what would it have to say*, by REUSING `engine/docs_scan.js` and `engine/quarantine.js` through their exports rather than recounting anything. It puts side by side the three different questions a figure can fail at a major, which is the thing that did not exist: each hand-count tonight was correct about its own question, and the one being quoted was whichever had been run last. Separately, `data/abra-tags.js` was rebuilt from `data/tags.json` after batch S/T moved the tag derivation and left the browser copy behind.
- **Measured.** **The living-document rewrite is 119 figure-level edits, not the 85–90 estimated by hand** — 13 resting on a withheld artifact, 84 stale citations, 22 untraceable, across the five documents in the major fold-in. A re-run today would **LIFT 40 artifacts and leave 24 withheld**, each of the 24 named with its generator. **45 of 100** notes rows owed; documents last folded at 5.266.0. Gate reads **CLOSED, 1 of 9** — narration. `engine/artifact_audit.js` now reads *"data/abra-tags.js is what build/build_tags_js.js would write from data/tags.json"*, no gaps found; it was a GAP that blocked a commit before the rebuild.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~roughly 85–90 figure-level edits~~ — superseded by **119**, derived. The earlier figure was my own hand estimate in `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-six-zero-zero-scope.md`, a dated findings record which is not rewritten. ~~39 lift / 22 stay~~ — superseded by **40 / 24** from the same derivation.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row. **OPEN AND NAMED:** releases cut between 02:54 and 03:55 froze a new `data/tags.json` beside the old `data/abra-tags.js`. Both are frozen SOURCES. Under node this changed nothing — `engine/tags.js:56` requires `tags.json` and the browser copy is only read on the browser path, so the measurements taken in that window stand — but a release re-cut is owed before 6.0.0 rests on a snapshot, and it was not done here because an agent was mid-run and cuts its own.

## [5.270.1] — 2026-09-09 — an ability no legal species can carry is a fact about the regulation, not a row in the roster
- **What changed.** `tests/roster.js`: the artifact's `results` array no longer carries rows tagged `no-legal-carrier`. Will, 2026-09-09: *"if its not in the format then toss it out and stop putting here"*. **The rows go; the count does not** — `scope.out_of_scope` and `scope.out_of_scope_by` are written from the same array BEFORE the filter and are derived every run, so the artifact still says how many were excluded and why. Dropping the count too would be the worse bug: a future session sees the stage fall from 316 rows to 202 and cannot tell whether coverage fell or noise was removed. The rows are tagged at the refusal by `cannot(why, 'no-legal-carrier')`, reached only when the legal-species walk returns empty — so the regulation decides this, not the file.
- **Measured.** **The abilities artifact goes 316 rows → 202**, and `COULD-NOT-STAGE` **158 → 44**, which was always the in-scope number. Verdicts now read 139 FIRED-AND-BOARDS-MATCH / 44 COULD-NOT-STAGE / 14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET / 5 DEFERRED-BY-OWNER = 202. `scope.out_of_scope` still reads **114 no-legal-carrier**. **The gate is unchanged** — `engine/status.js` reads deliberate roster / abilities PASS at *139 of 202 tested*, exactly as before, and still 1 of 9 clauses failing. Only the abilities stage was affected: moves, items and spine carry 0 out-of-scope rows.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure moves — the gate reads the same denominator it read before, which is the point: the 202 was always the real one and 316 was the number being printed at it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Still owed and unchanged by this row:** the 14 CONTROL-NOT-QUIET abilities, where the control arm is itself live and the delta cannot be charged to the entity. Those are the instrument's debt, not the regulation's.

## [5.270.0] — 2026-09-09 — narration batch S: the `-fail` family is four mechanisms, and one of them is the corpse rule for a third time
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` plus two derived params in `engine/tag_dex.js`; four mechanisms, four new probes, four restore knobs. (1) **A `-fail` the handler writes itself NAMES the move** — `allyswitch.onHit` (`data/moves.ts:326`) and `substitute.onTryHit` (`:18314`) announce their own refusal and return `NOT_FAIL`, so one line reaches the wire carrying the move name and, for the threshold refusal, `[weak]` in field 4; this engine wrote the generic two-field line for both. `tests/probe_fail_names_the_move.js`, knob `MEDI_BARE_FAIL_LABELS=1`. (2) **Champions gives Dire Claw a refusal line mainline does not have** — the mod rewrites the whole secondary (`data/mods/champions/moves.ts:191-209`) and announces on an already-statused target, naming the status when the roll matches it; Tri Attack is NOT overridden and refuses silently. `tests/probe_direclaw_refusal_line.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_PROCEDURAL_REFUSAL_LINE=1`. (3) **Two refusals this engine already made and made in SILENCE** — a Yawn at a statused body and a Leech Seed at a seeded one, each a silent conjunct in a guard rather than a branch with a consequence; the seed road also skipped the authority's 90% accuracy roll, so the test moved BELOW the die. `tests/probe_refusal_this_engine_swallowed.js`, knob `MEDI_SWALLOW_REFUSALS=1`. - **Measured.** **Gate NARRATION 33 → 21 of 961**, NARRATION-ONLY **32 → 22 causes / 34 → 22 games**, protocol divergence **37 → 25** — `data/game-differential.json`, release `5381b07ea2fa`, n=961 games, `--steering empirical --arm middle --end-state --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --turns 50`, census 830 rows, pool `0d103fb9fa87`, against release `2c4e125866cc`. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958, unmoved, checked after BOTH measurements.** Zero transfers at either step. `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses failing** — NARRATION and nothing else. Census 830 live / 0 missing / 830 probed (`data/mechanics-census.json`); all four roster stages 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE with 18/18, 44/44, 36/36 and 17/17 anchors live; `tests/test-engine-diff.js` 6000 agreed / 0 disagreed; `engine/all_mechanics_fire.js` 1313 games, 0 threw.

They stood only in `docs/ENGINE.md`'s batch R section, which is a dated record and is not rewritten.

## [5.269.1] — 2026-09-09 — a bibliography is where a document is MOST traceable, and the untraceable census was counting it as the opposite
- **What changed.** `engine/docs_scan.js`: two strips in `figuresIn` — an arXiv id and a DOI are citation IDENTIFIERS, not measurements. `ID_WORD` could not carry it, because its lookbehind demands a SPACE and both are written with a colon; that is the identical reason `§` and `#` were each given a strip here after sitting in `ID_WORD` without ever firing. Three cases added to `LEXING_CASES`, which the file requires in both directions: two that must NOT be read, and a control asserting that a real figure standing beside a citation still IS — without it the strips could be satisfied by discarding the whole line.
- **Measured.** **Untraceable figures 33 → 23, and the documents carrying them 5 → 3** — `node engine/docs_scan.js`. `docs/SLOWKING-whitepaper.md` (7) and `docs/ROLE-FAMILY.md` (2) fall to zero and leave the census entirely; `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md` drops 1. All ten were reference entries: `arXiv:2007.13544`, `DOI:10.1126/sciadv.adg3256` and five more in one bibliography, plus `arXiv 2304.08272` cited in two other documents. **The figure lexer demonstration goes 7/7 → 10/10 cases, all holding**, and `tests/test-docs-current.js` is unchanged at 33 passed, 0 failed.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** ~~untraceable figures 33 across 5 documents~~ — superseded by **23 across 3**, and the 10 were never figures. Stated in `docs/_reports/2026-09-09-six-zero-zero-scope.md`, a dated findings record which is not rewritten.
- **Owed to the next major.** none from this row. **Named rather than fixed:** 7 of the remaining 23 are figures quoted INSIDE their own WITHDRAWN/CORRECTED notice in `docs/MODELS.md` and `docs/ABRA-whitepaper.md`. They are the record working as designed and need no action at 6.0.0, but they inflate this counter and the retraction registry does not know their values, so exempting them is a design question and was deliberately not attempted here.

## [5.269.0] — 2026-09-08 — narration batch R: five mechanisms, sixteen NARRATION-ONLY causes, and a corpse writes no line
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js`, five mechanisms, five new probes, five restore knobs. (1) **A non-permanent forme revert is SILENT** — Champions' own `clearVolatile` closes with `setSpecies(baseSpecies)`, which emits nothing, and its `formeChange` sets `formeRegression` in the Tera branch ALONE (champions 1 assignment, mainline 2), so Stance Change reaches neither branch; this engine paid the revert through `formeSwap`, whose job is to announce one. `tests/probe_forme_revert_silent.js`, knob `MEDI_FORME_REVERT_ANNOUNCES=1`. (2) **A frozen body killed by its own thawing hit is not cured** — `Pokemon#cureStatus` opens `if (!this.hp || !this.status) return false;` (`sim/pokemon.ts:1680-1681`); `_thawCure` re-read the STATUS and its own comment claimed a corpse carries `'fnt'`, which this engine does not write. `tests/probe_thaw_on_a_corpse.js`, knob `MEDI_THAW_CURES_A_CORPSE=1`. (3) **An ability's clamped stat change still announces at magnitude zero** — `Battle#boost`'s zero branch announces for an Ability effect when the call is `isSecondary || isSelf`, and `effect ||= this.effect` is why Defiant, Competitive and Gooey reach it despite passing `null`; two opt-in sites had never opted in. `tests/probe_ability_zero_boost_line.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_ABILITY_ZERO_BOOST=1`. (4) **The absorbed gift's `-end` on the switch-out road**, which `abRewrite` had implemented for the rewrite road only and whose own comment said the switch road "already empties `_vol` wholesale" — it does, silently. `tests/probe_absorb_gift_end_on_leave.js`, knob `MEDI_NO_ABSORB_GIFT_END_ON_LEAVE=1`. (5) **The shield answers before the ability on the pivot road** — `moveSteps` puts `hitStepTryHitEvent` two steps above `hitStepTryImmunity`, and inside that one event handlers are collected volatiles-before-ability, so Protect speaks and Good as Gold and the Prankster refusal do not. `tests/probe_pivot_shield_before_ability.js`, knob `MEDI_PIVOT_ABILITY_BEFORE_SHIELD=1`.

Census **830 live / 830 probed** unchanged (`data/mechanics-census.json`); roster **142 / 139 / 487** with 0 DIFFER and 0 DID-NOT-FIRE and every anchor live (`data/roster.{items,abilities,moves}.json`); `test-engine-diff` **6000 agreed / 0 disagreed** (`data/engine-diff.json`). `engine/status.js` reads **1 of 9 gate clauses fail**, the NARRATION one.

Each of the four blocks now carries a dated SUPERSEDED note in place; the 5.249.0 figures beside it are left standing as the dated record they are. ## [5.268.1] — 2026-09-08 — the rotation checklist derived its blast radius from files that READ the config, so a typed format id was invisible to it by construction
- **What changed.** `engine/next_regulation.js --checklist` gains a step, and the predicate behind it moves to a new `engine/format_id_scan.js` that `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js` now requires instead of restating. The checklist derives what a regulation flip touches from files that read `data/regulations.json` or take the format from `champions_sim`. **A file with the id TYPED into it does neither**, so it could not appear in a radius whose whole value is completeness — it keeps working after the flip, about the previous regulation, and reports success. Two defects in the probe fell out of the refactor and are fixed: `execFileSync` ran at the default 1 MB `maxBuffer` and threw `ENOBUFS` on the two largest files it scans, and it ignored git stderr, so a brand-new file and a real git failure were indistinguishable and it was calling the former the latter.
- **Measured.** The red arm is the command own output: before this change `--checklist` named **zero** typed format ids; it now prints **35 live call site(s) across 33 file(s)**, derived at run time from the working tree, naming `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`. `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js` is unchanged in verdict — **GREEN, 0 live call sites across 233 .js files under `engine/` and `build/`**, which are the gate — and its BEFORE side is now complete at **0 git failures** where it reported 1 and then 3. Reg M-C **does not exist yet**: `engine/next_regulation.js` reads 4 Champions VGC formats from the live authority and none sorts after `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`, so nothing here is owed tonight.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. No published figure moves — this is a procedure and a scanner, and the one count it prints (35) never existed before.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. The 35 sites are **a decision per site at the flip, not a find-and-replace**: a format id under `tests/` is usually a pin on what was MEASURED, and rewriting those would be editing the record.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — five Regulation M-C rotation seams that would each have failed at exit 0, closed red-first
- **What changed.** `engine/analyze.js` filters the usage model on the store's per-row format token and REFUSES to write a model when no stored row is the active regulation. `engine/durable-ingest.js` factors the token derivation out of `extract()` into exported `formatToken()` / `activeStoreFormat()` — the latter deriving the token twice, from the label and from the Showdown id, and throwing when they disagree — and gains a `STALE-LADDER` discriminator so a ROTATED ladder is no longer indistinguishable from a quiet one. `engine/artifact_audit.js` gains check **H**: the format is asked whether every legal `megaStone` forme has a row carrying `bs`, which is the field `buildMon` gates on. `build/build_browser_data.js`, `engine/derive_switch_carry.js`, `engine/fixture_preflight.js` and `engine/mega_harvest.js` take their format through `champions_sim` instead of naming M-B. `engine/next_regulation.js --checklist` prints the flip procedure with its blast radius derived on every run. `.github/workflows/ingest.yml`'s pull-failure message names both discriminators instead of asserting a broken endpoint. New probes: `tests/probe_usage_regulation_pool.js`, `tests/probe_new_mega_row.js`, `tests/probe_rotated_ladder.js`, `tests/probe_format_id_derivation.js`.
- **Measured.** NO PUBLISHED FIGURE MOVES. The pooling filter excludes **0 of 76,833** rows — `data/games.ladder.jsonl` is 76,833 of 76,833 `champions-regmb`, `data/games.bo3.jsonl` 25,522 of 25,522. Check H reads **76 of 76** legal mega formes present with base stats and `engine/artifact_audit.js` exits 0, no gaps. The extractor refactor re-parses **300 raw logs from `data/games.ladder.raw-logs.jsonl`: 300 agree, 0 disagree** against the stored rows, and `tests/test-parse.js` is 42 of 42. Red arms, each reproduced before the fix and refused after: pooling **2,406 → 2,324 team-sides** (82 sides of Reg M-A, from the 51 real rows in `data/games.gen9championsvgc2026regmabo3.jsonl`, inside a model labelled `gen9championsvgc2026regmb`; top three species moved 0.5–0.6 points); a legal mega with no row **exit 0 with 0 GAP → exit 1 with 1 GAP naming it**; a rotated ladder **exit 0 and silent → exit 1 STALE-LADDER**, with the QUIET control still exit 0 and the DEAD control still owned by ZERO-GAIN; format ids typed into a live call under `engine/` and `build/` **5 at HEAD → 0 in the tree**. The rotation bar is measured, not assumed: the live pool held 1,275 ids, newest 0.02 h old, spanning 22.50 h, and the 24 h floor is set against this store's 40.7 games/hour over its last 14 days.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Owed now:** `data/meta-usage.json` was deliberately NOT regenerated while an ENGINE agent was live, so it does not yet carry the two new provenance fields — one command, `node engine/analyze.js data/games.ladder.jsonl`, and no figure in it changes. `tests/test-quality.js` is red on a PRE-EXISTING clause untouched by this work (`clean share is stable: 31.3% now vs 28.0% recorded`, because the store grew 67,384 → 76,833). Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-mc-seam-repairs.md`.
- **Still open after this pass, stated rather than filed.** The oracle pin becomes `null` (UNKNOWN, never false) under an npm install and `sim/package.json` carries a caret range; a rotation does not move `data/mechanics-census.json`, so `engine/steering.js` keeps aiming the differential at M-B mechanics; `engine/names.js` `megaTable()` holds **74 entries against 76 legal mega formes**, losing one of Charizard-Mega-X/Y and one of Raichu-Mega-X/Y to a base-keyed table — filed, not fixed, because it is a play-path file; 34 format ids under `tests/` are pins on measured runs and become a decision on the day of the flip, scheduled by nothing.

## [5.268.0] — 2026-09-08 — the post-action re-sort ran above the Update pass, so a mid-turn White Herb handed Unburden its doubling one action too late
- **What changed.** `engine/medicham2-browser.js` — the post-action re-sort `_resortTail(actIdx)` moves from ABOVE `_updateAll()` to BELOW it, which is where the authority puts it: the re-sort is the LAST statement of `Battle#runAction` (`sim/battle.ts:2915-2922`) and `eachEvent('Update')` is at `:2856`, so an `onUpdate` item is spent BEFORE the remaining queue is sorted. A White Herb spent by the Update pass that closes a PIVOT SWITCH therefore reached the sort in Showdown and not here. New counter `MEDSEEN.queueResortBelowUpdate`; `MEDI_RESORT_BEFORE_UPDATE=1` restores the old placement verbatim. `tests/probe_midturn_herb_resort.js` — new, cast derived from `data/tags.json` and the format, three arms (REAL / SILENT / knob-child), every fixture inequality checked against the authority's own `getActionSpeed()` readings. `docs/ENGINE.md` gains the pass and its hand list.
- **Measured.** **`order_probe` rows carrying `speed_tied:false` AND `same_priority:true`: 1 → 0**, and `node engine/quarantine.js --order-probe` **exit 2 → exit 0** — `data/game-differential.json`, releases `7f012a9afe01` → `f6f44b329132`, `--steering empirical --arm middle --games 1200 --team-store data/team-pool-frozen --end-state --write`, census `886d47cf7fef` (830 rows, CREDITED ONLY under empirical steering), pins `de38d17e15a2`, cap 50, n=961 games each. **BOARD-MATERIAL 0 of 958 → 0 of 958**, 10,675 of 10,675 turn boundaries identical. **NARRATION-ONLY 53 → 52 of 961** across 51 causes. The `ordering` class falls **14 → 13 games** with the other thirteen causes byte-identical and nothing new in either direction. `data/mechanics-census.json` **830 live / 830 probed, `run_ok true`, unchanged**. All three roster stages re-run on `f6f44b329132` (`tests/roster.js --stage {items,abilities,moves} --reds --write`): `data/roster.items.json` **142**, `data/roster.abilities.json` **139**, `data/roster.moves.json` **487**, every count identical to the pre-fix run and **0 DIFFER, 0 DID-NOT-FIRE on all three**.
- **Basis.** unchanged.
- **Supersedes.** Nothing. The row was declared OPEN and gating; no figure was published from it.
- **Owed to the next major.** none. **Owed now:** (a) `orderProbeClause`'s rerun hint in `engine/quarantine.js` omits `--end-state`, which is sample-defining per `game_differential.js:200-207` — it cost one 13-minute run in this pass and is reported, not changed; (b) 517 speed readings disagree in 223 of 961 games, every one carrying `status=-/sd:fnt` (the authority's body fainted where this engine's is not), parting no board — not investigated and claimed neither way; (c) `ability/priority-mod`'s delivery move is still not learnset-aware. Full account: `docs/_reports/2026-09-08-gap-286-ordering.md`.
- **Correction to a premise, recorded because it was carried into the brief.** Gale Wings was believed *"never once exercised"* on the strength of a `COULD-NOT-STAGE` in `data/roster.abilities.json`. It is covered by **three live rows in `data/mechanics-census.json`**, including the exact full-HP control — *"Gale Wings puts a full-HP Flying move in front, and not a chipped one"* (`full HP 0`, `at 60% 60`) and *"the Gale Wings body ONE HP below full was refused false / landed true"*. A refusal by ONE instrument was read as an absence across all of them; the roster debt is real and is about its delivery table, not about the mechanic.
- **Which engine was wrong, established rather than assumed.** Under the knob the two engines agree on EVERY boundary speed reading (0 disagreeing, 0 desyncs) and the order still parts — so both put the same number in the sort key and medicham2 was wrong about the moment, not the number. Trick Room, Gale Wings and Prankster were each refuted for this row by measurement: Champions NEGATES for Trick Room (`data/mods/champions/scripts.ts:45-53`) and the row's speeds are POSITIVE (664, 378).
- **Honest reading of the green.** `--order-probe` now passes *"clean by absence: 961 games produced NO move-vs-move ordering pair to probe."* The differential proves the ROW is gone; what proves the MECHANIC is the probe, which stages the fixture deliberately and carries a knob-cleared control.
